</div>
<h2>History by venue</h2>
<p class="gm-method">The full held history for each venue, in the venue's own unit. Series longer than 280 reports print as yearly extremes over the full span plus the most recent 104 reports; shorter series print in full. Every table sorts and filters.</p>

<h2>Coverage and method</h2>
<p class="gm-method">The CFTC aluminium lines are premium contracts and a thin flagship; the LME high-grade line is the European centre. The Shanghai leg joins when the SHFE dataset lands.</p>
<p class="gm-method">Sector: Metals, Base metals. Units print per row and are never converted. Category definitions are each source's own. Pages render the current corrected vintage of each source; first-release vintages are not kept. An extreme reading is a condition of crowding, not a buy or sell signal.</p>
